Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the math_analysis structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 822. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 940 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the math_analysis space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 822 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 940. QED.
